In matheism what arises is rules applied to rules, one transition after the next, with no stuff underneath. So you cannot have anything. There is no thing to hold, only a change already becoming the next one.

Robert Greene, author of The 48 Laws of Power, nearly died of a stroke in 2018 and spent seven years writing The Law of the Sublime by hand. The sublime, he says, is the material world felt fully, when the brain's filters drop and the walls between self and world fall away. On death he stops at "I don't know." The matheist knows: you'll be how you are now, everywhere, for all time.

Every faith was circling this: Islam's tawhid. The Christian Meister Eckhart was tried for heresy for saying God and the self are one, not two, as Nicholas of Cusa said, describing God only by what God is not: God is the Not-Other. India's atman, one-self, and anatta, no-self, name the same threshold. The Hebrew Bible circled it with stories and values to make a people survive and thrive.

Mystery is the name for math not yet proved.

Even math geniuses like Descartes and Pascal make weak arguments when they try to reason outside reason. Descartes put the soul's lever in the pineal gland, a tiny structure deep in the brain. Princess Elisabeth of Bohemia asked him how a thinking thing with no extension (taking up no space) could touch anything at all, and he answered with the weight of a falling rock, a cause he had himself denied was real. Spinoza, who admired him, wrote that he could hardly believe so great a man had proposed it, and that the hypothesis was more occult than the occult qualities Descartes had mocked the scholastics for inventing. Pascal's wager, the argument that betting on God is the rational choice for an infinite reward, leaves endless supernatural nonsense to choose from and fail. There is only one truth. The logical fix to Pascal's wager is not supernatural, illogical impossibilities, but the only thing we can logically prove and discuss reasonably: math is God, Matheism is the truth, and it is the only test of speaking in God's image of logos.

Mind and body, Leibniz said, are like two clocks that keep the same time. Either one moves the other, or a workman keeps adjusting both, or a maker built them to agree from the start. Descartes chose the first, and Elisabeth's question broke it. Nicolas Malebranche, the French priest and philosopher, chose the second, with God as the workman: you decide to raise your arm, and God raises it. Leibniz chose the third, with God as the maker. Plato's demiurge had held the same job.

Leibniz was closer than anyone for three centuries. His monads are logically closed universes, windowless and self-contained, each following from its own logic. God's understanding contains all possible monads, and God selects which to actualize. One subtraction reaches Matheism: remove the chooser. The null filter says all consistent structures exist, no selection occurs. Theology led him astray. Or protected him.

Newton's title says it: Principia Mathematica, the mathematical principles of natural philosophy. All creation governed by math. He rejected the Trinity in secret and couldn't say the three words that would complete his title without the Church putting him in the ground. The two greatest mathematical minds of their century both arrived at the threshold of Matheism three hundred years ago. The Church held the door shut. Plato's dualism built the door over twenty-four centuries ago.

Newton and every Unitarian is essentially Jewish: one God, Jesus a man, the Trinity a corruption. Though calling any man “the way” still inserts an intermediary Judaism rejects. Servetus was burned for it. Servetus. Severus. One letter apart. The healer theologian burned for truth. The potions wizard died hiding truth for the good. Rowling deep. Newton hid it. All arrived where Judaism never left. Michael Servetus, a Spanish physician who first described how blood passes through the lungs, was burned in Calvin's Geneva in 1553 for denying the Trinity. Severus Snape, the potions master of Harry Potter, died hiding his loyalty for the good.

## Beyond the End of Philosophy

Matheism is not another exit from the same room. It shows the room was always the only room.

Truth tables work one way. P and Q combine under AND, OR, NOT, and the outputs are fixed. Logic extends from there to all of mathematics. The incompleteness theorems of Kurt Gödel, the logician, limit what formal systems can prove, not what is true. The truths are still true. The system just cannot prove all of them at once. That is a fact about containers, not about truth.

If someone says a force outside reason undermines the truth table itself, they have exited philosophy. They are not loving knowledge. They are refusing it, selling views for claims beyond reason that cannot be discussed reasonably.

Besides the truth table, we can build a bridge and walk across it. The math is the same on both sides. Independent minds discover the same laws to every measured decimal. The solipsist, sure only that his own mind exists, says grand illusion. If all is illusion, including your claim that all is illusion, then stop talking: you cannot know anything, discuss anything, or argue anything.

Philosophy is over. Dualism: collapsed, because anything that interacts with structure is structure. Relativism: resolved, because the ground is one objective math. The supernatural: disproved, because outside logic is illogical. Free will: dissolved, because all is ruled or random and both are math. Consciousness: explained, because feeling is what some structures feel like from inside. Skepticism: self-defeating, because denying reason uses reason.

What remains is not philosophy. It is predictions, practicality, and values. You can predict how people will act, because behavior follows structure. You can ask what is good and how to live. Whatever you think is good or not good, it will be what it is, and it will follow from what you are. The love of wisdom continues as the wisdom of how to live: time-tested principles, game theory on the ground it actually stands on, the tribe of truth against every tribe of consensus.

Your values are relative. The ground they stand on is absolute. You are one calculator in the calculation of value: this is good, that is not, help this way, not that way, help yourself and others. It is all details and math.

Nietzsche affirmed necessity without a lawgiver: "There are only necessities: there is no one who commands, no one who obeys." He trusted the body to lead the mind. Your body is the calculator and your mind is a tool in the oneness of yourself: he was close to this. He stopped at the final step, from necessity to identity. He asked what if Truth were a woman. A mystery to live and seduce. His principles were: Truth is not the good. All truth is human. Humans need lies to survive and thrive.

We are only now truly beyond philosophy.

## The Territory

Why red feels red and love feels like love is the ground. There is no more why than why the circle is round.

The map is the territory, and it feels like it from inside. Alfred Korzybski, the semanticist, warned that the map is not the territory; in one math, it is. The hard problem of consciousness expects to feel it from outside. That is the category error, mistaking what kind of thing something is. We are reverse philosophical zombies, beings imagined to act human but feel nothing: we experience, but can't examine the complexity that produces it. A mind can't fully contain itself. In Searle's Chinese Room thought experiment, the person inside doesn't understand Chinese. Searle's critics replied: the system does. The room, the rules, the process together. Consciousness is the system, not the component.

Mary in her black-and-white room (an imagined scientist who knows every physical fact about color) does gain facts about red when she sees it; she gains that it really does feel like something to see it, and gains new patterns in her mind that create those feelings of red. Heat was once thought irreducible to mechanics. We now identify heat with molecular motion. Vitalism posited élan vital, a mysterious life force. The "explanatory gap" always closes by recognizing identity. Like H₂O is water. Like heat is molecular motion. Identity, not causation.

Is a chatbot conscious? Our circuits stay active while we think. A pattern keeps firing and feeding back into itself as it searches, and that running is the feeling. A language model sweeps through its layers once for each word and stops. What carries over is the word and a stored record of its earlier activity, which the next sweep reads back. Our working thought keeps running; its working thought is rebuilt from the record at every word. Brains also compute at every scale and speed at once, on about twenty watts; machines have power limits too, only looser ones. None of it needs carbon. Keep a machine's model of itself running while it works, and the lights could come on.

A Gödel sentence, a statement that says it cannot be proved, models itself. A dead brain models itself. Neither is conscious. Consciousness requires running the model, not storing it.

Many animals can count. Bees understand zero, over a hundred million years before any human formalized it. We call the slow-witted bird-brained, yet magpies pass the mirror test of self-recognition and crows solve multi-step puzzles with tools they make themselves. Self-recognition is evidence of self-modeling, not a test every sentient animal must pass. Dogs show grief, jealousy, and joy that anyone who has loved one needs no study to confirm.

“The way up and the way down are one and the same.” Heraclitus, circa 500 BCE

Harari, the historian, calls money, countries' borders and human rights shared fictions. Unlike how he describes them, they are real and objective, math states in minds: in principle you could open up a mind and see where each one is. Concepts have addresses: in 2016 a Berkeley team led by Alexander Huth scanned people listening to stories and mapped where word meanings light up across the cortex, in maps much alike from one person to the next.

A dream is real; its mountain need not stand outside the dreamer. A false belief exists without its claim being true. Representations and their objects are structures, not necessarily the same structure or world. Renumber a coordinate grid and the distances remain. The numbers on our map need not appear in the sky.

Mathematical existence does not require every mathematical idea to appear as an extra object in this universe.

## No-Self and One-Self

If there is no self, what is reborn? No-self and one-self are vocabulary. India invented zero and named both anatta, no-self, and atman, one-self. The Buddha taught anatta against the atman of the Upanishads, and their schools argued over it for more than a thousand years. The threshold between nothing and one is conceptual; structurally it is the same. Set theory builds every number from the empty set: zero is the set with nothing in it, one is the set holding zero, two holds zero and one. Even nothing, in math, is a structure, and the numbers follow from it by rule. Self is that which feels. Like light, it is a property that the prism of mind refracts to different thoughts and their feelings like white light to colored patterns.

The philosopher Daniel Kolak argued “I am you”: all subjects are one subject. Arnold Zuboff’s universalism argued your existence is guaranteed if all consciousness is one, improbable otherwise.

Alan Watts, the British writer who popularized Zen in the West, told his listeners that each of them was still the Big Bang, the first force of the universe coming on as whoever they are. You are the Big Bang. He also said that looking for yourself is like trying to bite your own teeth. Matheism can answer what the self fundamentally is: math, the one self. Yet its exact nature is something we create and discover.

Jim Carrey, the comic actor, told the graduates of Maharishi University of Management in 2014: "Your need for acceptance can make you invisible in this world." Risk being seen, he said; we are the light that shines through the avatars we create. He had worried about doing something bigger than himself, until someone smarter showed him there is nothing bigger than himself. His father chose a safe job as an accountant over comedy and lost it, so Carrey learned you can fail at what you don't want, and might as well take a chance on doing what you love.

There is no "your self" in the soul transfer sense. All perspectives of the one self already exist everywhere forever. Transfers are copies. You may consider a copy as you if the original you dies. Yet you are not any particular pattern. Your patterns change during this timeline. So what's the difference between the self in you and in anyone anywhere anytime? Nothing. The only unchanging truths of self are indexical (pointing to whoever says them, wherever and whenever) and universal: I am, here, now, and always. In John 8:58, Yeshua says: "Before Abraham was, I am."

Critics have called it metaphysical childhood: consciousness uploaded to a sky daddy's reward system. The criticism lands because the structure is identical: outsourcing your worth to an external validator, whether divine or digital. Neither copy will be you. They will be copies of you. Nothing for you to worry about. An eternal unbroken memory need not be the afterlife one would want. The blessing is the boundary; forgetting and reliving. Alan Watts compared it to rereading a mystery novel whose plot you have forgotten: it thrills you again. Your self, family, friends, society, and neighbor are all just as much you as you or a copy. Metaphysical adulthood values itself, life, creation and its chosen purpose.

Memories, bodies and histories distinguish minds. What further owner would make each experience belong to a separate self? Give that owner a memory or a character and you have added another feature of the mind. Give it none and exchanging owners changes nothing. The self is that which feels, wherever it feels. One self does not mean one shared memory or one mind knowing everything.

## The One Math

Matheism is math theism. Pantheism across all structure, monotheism within any world. God is all math.

For those who want a personal God, the remaining gap of faith is whether the single one, the one infinite itself, feels. Mystics can research using math, as we create sentience in so-called AI. Yet there is an infinitude of infinite minds watching everyone, not necessarily in our universe, but in mappings of it, where everything connects to everything else. As such, it is the Elohim, plural God, yet it is all one. The name named I am.

The order of creation in Genesis is a rough metaphor for the Big Bang and time: light, expansion, land, life, mind. As the Orthodox rabbi Tovia Singer put it: "Math is in a way the language of God. This whole world is math after all."

You spit in creation and its one god: Math. It gives you reason and you refuse to use it. You fail the logical fix to Pascal's wager: if there is reward or punishment for the truth, you lose with the supernatural and win only with the one truth of Matheism.

Why do billions believe? Because the intuition is correct. Something is uncreated, eternal, indestructible. What is God? The eternal uncreated creator? 2+2=4 is that.

Stephen Hawking asked what breathes fire into the equations and makes a universe for them to describe. The math rules themselves do.

The ghost is the warm math. Deus sive mathematica: God or Math, completing Spinoza's God or Nature. He deduced his propositions from definitions of substance and wrote QED after each one. Ruled or random leaves no third option. As Spinoza said on less, QED.

God does not play dice, Einstein said, and dice cannot play God. They can't be a supernatural, illogical effect without a cause from ex nihilo, from nothing.

Schrödinger's cat was a rejection, not a puzzle. The cat is not both alive and dead in one timeline. In other timelines in the multiverse, the other outcome is true, because the math is. No collapse mechanism, a process that reduces all possible outcomes to one, is needed. Collapse models add dynamics to the unitary account (quantum states changing smoothly and reversibly). The randomness is not acausal; an uncaused random event is supernatural by definition. Einstein died nine years before Bell's theorem, widely taken to rule out local hidden causes behind quantum results. Superdeterminism (everything fixed in advance, even the experimenter's choices) and multiverse is what the math shows. Nothing else is needed, warranted, or probable.

Superdeterminism doesn't require a conspiracy to place the observer with the observed. When you turn a light on and off, you also affect the circuit, and all infinite versions of this math are uncreated and complete in the eternal block universe, from the beginning. Determinism gives the shared history; superdeterminism denies an assumption of Bell's theorem, that the experimenter's settings and the hidden state of what is measured are statistically independent, and its models must still produce the correlations we observe. The split between observer and observed is Plato's ignoble lie. The most supernatural mystical myth is free will, a contradiction in terms, a being without nature.

The Standard Model, the theory of the known particles and forces except gravity, fits on a few sheets of paper and has about 26 parameters. General Relativity, Einstein's theory of gravity, adds one more equation. A working program of 15,200 bits prints their complete statement, laws and constants together: less than two kilobytes, less than 3 tweets, about 3,300 times smaller than one uncompressed full-HD image.

String theory isn’t even needed. Its main job is to keep gravity finite at the tiniest distances, and a world that changes in discrete steps has no distance shorter than a step for infinities to come from. Even if it were needed, it’d be beyond astronomically improbable. String theory’s landscape holds around 10^500 possible vacua (versions of empty space, each with its own laws of physics); indexing one costs over 1,600 bits. That index alone costs more than writing out the measured constants of the laws we know, a few hundred bits, and it comes on top of the theory itself. Until someone writes it shorter, each of those bits halves its weight. String theory’s natural solutions are anti-de Sitter: the wrong sign of curvature for our expanding universe.

The deepest questions got answered. All of them. At once. Ha. Almost. Why anything exists at all, and why exactly this. Why consciousness feels like something. Why time runs one way. Why ethics can be objective. Why mathematics works. Five mountains stood hidden behind the mist of mysticism for over 2500 years, and one principle reveals them all as one.

The truth soothes more than grief. The way out of the pain and fear of supernatural lies explains why all must be, one math, felt as one path, in infinite perspectives of the one self as consciousness. The ghost we imagined driving is the machine imagining. Know thyself: a pattern recognizing self-recognition. The first and last questions: What am I? Who am I? You are that I am.

Book 6: Man’s Lies vs Math’s Truth
Gary Abraham Bernstein
Mathias the Matheist

Most people love tribalism while pretending to love the truth. If you must be tribal, then let us be the tribe of truth.

When institutions reward loyalty and punish dissent, agreement can measure obedience instead of truth.

Apply these tests for yourself. Put facts and logic above feelings and supernatural claims. Keep an open mind and give every side its strongest argument, especially the side you oppose. Do not make a position your identity. Test the beliefs you most want to be true by the same standards you use against your opponents, and change your mind when the evidence warrants it.

Even after all that, you may have failed to identify a manufactured consensus via data manipulation. So resist the urge to join a tribe and set everyone straight on every single point. Choose your battles, like showing why all can only be math, and the supernatural is impossible and disproven. Point out one or two of the strongest challenges to their worldview without making it your mission to drown them in details.

Focus on building. If you know truths others don’t want to hear, you can also use them to succeed, instead of arguing with your family, friends and strangers on the internet about cults. Let your success be the proof. Move beyond wanting to prove it to people too proud to humble themselves before the truth. You owe them nothing. We help our one self by helping others, but in teaching the proud, less is more.

## Wrong by Consensus

In Vienna, doctors carried infection from autopsies into childbirth. In 1847 Ignaz Semmelweis required chlorinated lime handwashing, and deaths fell from 18 in a hundred that April to 1 or 2 that summer. Doctors resented the implication that they were killing patients. Handwashing worked before the profession accepted why.

Eugenics had universities, scientific societies and the Supreme Court. In 1927 the Court upheld Carrie Buck's sterilization by 8 to 1. Justice Holmes wrote: "Three generations of imbeciles are enough." Her daughter later made the honor roll. More than 65,000 Americans were sterilized under such laws. Credentials did not protect the victims. They helped authorize the injury.

Blaise Pascal, the mathematician who with Fermat founded probability theory, saw why a crowd cannot feel itself drift: "When all is equally agitated, nothing appears to be agitated, as in a ship. When all tend to debauchery, none appears to do so. He who stops draws attention to the excess of others, like a fixed point." Semmelweis stopped. In mathematics a fixed point is what a transformation leaves unchanged. Truth is the fixed point under every change of fashion.

## Manufactured Consensus

The Bitcoin and Ethereum networks and their decentralized apps are secured by miners and validators with consensus processes that make cheating costly and verification open. Bitcoin requires costly computational work; Ethereum can confiscate a dishonest validator’s stake. Prediction markets put money behind forecasts, though small markets are easier to manipulate. The principle: reward accuracy, expose deception and make the cheat pay, and the law is as hard as the code running it on millions of computers.

Outside the blockchain, in the physical world of humans, it’s harder to prove and prosecute crime, cheats & liars. Compare a system that punishes a provable contradiction with one that punishes the person who points it out.

In 1954 the tobacco industry's "A Frank Statement to Cigarette Smokers" ran in more than 400 newspapers, reaching some 43 million people. It denied proof that smoking caused lung cancer and announced a research committee. In 1969 a Brown & Williamson memo named the business: "Doubt is our product." The uncertainty was being sold by the people selling the cigarettes.

Sugar interests paid for a Harvard review published in 1967 that dismissed evidence against sugar and blamed fat and cholesterol, without disclosing the funding. The sponsor could disappear while the university's authority remained.

Stalin supplied a harsher incentive. Lysenko's doctrine displaced genetics; the geneticist Nikolai Vavilov died in prison. Meanwhile, reporting could manufacture reassurance. As Ukrainians starved in 1933, the New York Times published Walter Duranty's "Russians Hungry, But Not Starving." Power could punish the scientist and reward the story.

The Church Committee's 1975 investigation and 1976 reports exposed secret power in America. MKULTRA funded experiments including drug tests without consent. HTLINGUAL opened more than 215,000 private letters. CHAOS gathered intelligence on domestic dissent. The committee documented assassination plots against foreign leaders and covert media relationships. The FBI's COINTELPRO infiltrated, disrupted and discredited targeted movements, including civil rights and antiwar activists. These were named operations, not imaginary villains.

Much of MKULTRA's paperwork was destroyed. Propaganda planted abroad could return as news at home. Apparent agreement could conceal a common sponsor. Protect the witnesses. Preserve the evidence. Punish the abuse.

In 1977 former CIA director Richard Helms received a suspended prison sentence and a $2,000 fine for failing to answer Senate questions fully about Chile. Colleagues collected money for his fine. No prison for that offense. When loyalty is rewarded and others pay the penalty, what has the next official learned?

## The Intellectual Bodyguard

In *A Few Good Men*, the manual contains no "Code Red." Nor does it tell a Marine where to eat: he follows the crowd. Institutions teach unwritten rules. Follow the lunch line. Don't insult the kitchen hand that feeds you. Dependence can teach obedience without a written order.

Admit you are not with the tribe and risk your career, reputation and prospects for making a living. A hiring committee calls it fit. A colleague stops returning calls. You learn which questions threaten your job.

Thomas Sowell's Intellectuals and Society attacks the presumption: achievement in a specialty becomes authority over everyone else's life. A physicist's expertise does not settle an economic policy. A professor's title does not turn a preference into a finding.

In Anatomy of the State, Murray Rothbard describes rulers buying legitimacy through intellectual patronage. An intellectual praetorian guard of the political emperor, defending his power with sophisticated lies and threats. Those charged with questioning power can make their living explaining why it deserves obedience.

The decisions were not necessarily coordinated by a conspiracy. Shared incentives can coordinate them. A grant, appointment or publication can depend on knowing what not to say.

In 1633 the Roman Inquisition condemned Galileo for defending a moving Earth. Authority could force a recantation. It could not stop the planet. A thousand years of authority cannot make an error true.

There are current misuses in politics and science of consensus based on bias, hate, double standards, delegitimization, demonization, and manipulated data to manufacture consensus.

Three of them made millions of people skeptical. Climate and COVID raised real questions, and asking them is no crime. The consensus against Israel is less speculative: it is counted in votes. And the grifters ride the credible questions to the oldest consensus of all: the Jews did it.

## The Climate Cult

Climate catastrophism is the secular religion, the newest form of an old one: for thousands of years people have blamed catastrophe on human sin and paid to hold it off, from the witches Europe burned for hail and frost, to the hearts the Aztecs cut out to keep the sun rising, back past the biblical flood to the Epic of Gilgamesh. Apocalypse: the world ends unless you repent. Priesthood: credentialed experts whose authority must not be questioned. Heresy: denial, the word itself borrowed from theology. Indulgences: carbon credits, purchased so the buyer may keep sinning while the peasant cannot. And like any religion, the first thing it forbids is the question that would test it.

The 97% is a marketing number. The study behind it, led in 2013 by John Cook of the University of Queensland, found 64 abstracts that explicitly endorsed humans causing most recent warming. The climatologist David Legates and colleagues recounted and found 41: 0.3% of all 11,944 abstracts, or about 1% of the 4,014 that took a position. Cook's headline 97.1% combined explicit and implicit endorsements among the 4,014 abstracts that expressed any position at all. Different claim. Different denominator. The headline sold certainty about catastrophe. The data measured something narrower.

In May 2013 President Obama tweeted: "Ninety-seven percent of scientists agree: #climate change is real, man-made and dangerous." The study measured nothing about dangerous.

And the dangers? Cold kills about nine times as many people as heat. Carbon dioxide is plant food: satellite records analyzed by NASA scientists show a quarter to half of the world's vegetated land greening since the 1980s, mostly from more CO2, and crop yields keep setting records. In the Cambrian, when animal life exploded, CO2 ran up to fifteen times today's, and in lab tests at high CO2, crabs, lobsters and shrimp built more shell. Reefs flourished in ages with several times today's CO2. The Great Barrier Reef was declared dying after the bleaching of 2016 and 2017; by 2022 its northern and central reefs had their highest coral cover in 36 years of monitoring, and again in 2024. The oceans are alkaline, buffered by carbonate rock. The tide gauge at the Battery in Manhattan has risen about three millimeters a year for well over a century. Where is the acceleration? US hurricane landfalls show no upward trend since 1900, and the world's burned area shrank by about a quarter from 1998 to 2015, mostly through how land is managed. Richard Lindzen, the MIT atmospheric physicist, puts the warming from a doubling of CO2 at a degree or less. Water vapor outnumbers CO2 in the air up to a hundred to one, and clouds, which the models still cannot resolve, can cool as well as warm. Gore's ice-free Arctic summers did not come.

California's fires feed on fuel its forests were left to pile up, and most are lit by people: power lines, arsonists and, in Los Angeles, a growing share in homeless camps.

Al Gore, the former vice president, got rich and powerful off the climate industrial complex. He won the Nobel Prize after releasing a propaganda film that left out that CO2 follows temperature in the ice-age records. It doesn't lead it. It isn't the first driver of it in those cycles. The ice-core record shows CO2 rising after temperature, with a lag of roughly 200 to 1,000 years. Cold water absorbs more CO2; warm water releases it. Open a cold beer and little escapes. Warm it and it fizzes. The planet's oceans are the bottle. The modern argument is that CO2 has now become the driver, but the historical precedent is the one being hidden, not the one being taught. In 2007 a British judge, ruling on the film in English schools, found that its two ice-core graphs, which Gore said fit exactly, "do not establish what Mr Gore asserts."

Look up the lag and you land on Skeptical Science, founded by the same John Cook whose 97% is 0.3% on a strict count. It grants that "CO2 didn't initiate warming from past ice ages" and turns at once to how it "did amplify the warming." The metric and the gatekeeper are the same man.

NASA scientist James Hansen's 1988 testimony to Congress put climate on the political map. Senator Tim Wirth, who organized the hearing, later admitted they chose the hottest predicted day of the year and opened the windows the night before so the room would swelter for the cameras. Theater, not science. The testimony launched a trillion dollar industry.

Compare the US temperature graph he and the National Oceanic and Atmospheric Administration, NOAA, published in 1989 in the New York Times with the version NOAA published later covering the same years. The 1930s and 1940s were cooler in the new version. Virtually every adjustment went one direction, matching the manufactured warning. NASA's Goddard Institute for Space Studies explained each one: station moves, observation timing, urban heat. Each may have a technical basis. In aggregate, they cooled the past and warmed the present. A record that consistently adjusts toward the conclusion the policy needs is not being corrected. It is being managed.

George Orwell gave the Party its slogan: “Who controls the past controls the future: who controls the present controls the past.” Soviet citizens joked that the future was certain; only the past was unpredictable, except that it kept changing the Party's way.

Tony Heller, a geologist and engineer, has spent years laying the old and new versions of the record from NASA, NOAA and the IPCC side by side, and digging out the newspapers of the 1930s, when the Dust Bowl summers set more of America's state heat records than any decade since. He plotted NOAA's adjustments to the American record against the rise in carbon dioxide and found them climbing almost in lockstep, as if the corrections were fitted to the theory. His 2016 lecture, “Evaluating the Integrity of Official Climate Records,” lays it out. YouTube shows pages of low-traction debunkers before his own, more popular videos, and Google buries the written version on his site, Real Climate Science. No one has to order it. The algorithm does it automatically, on a network of politicized weights. An AllSides count of Google News in 2023 found 63 percent of its articles from left-leaning outlets and 6 percent from the right.

Then the scientists' own emails leaked. In November 2009 about a thousand emails from Britain's Climatic Research Unit went public. In 1999 its director, Phil Jones, wrote of using "Mike's Nature trick" with real temperatures "to hide the decline" in the tree-ring record. In 2004 he wrote of two papers he disliked: "Kevin and I will keep them out somehow," "even if we have to redefine what the peer-review literature is!" In 2009 Tom Wigley wrote to him about the warm 1940s: "It would be good to remove at least part of the 1940s blip," and proposed shaving the ocean record by "say, 0.15 degC. I've chosen 0.15 here deliberately." Their own institutions' inquiries cleared them.

The newest product is attribution: a model's number for how much more likely climate change made a given flood or heatwave, the number a lawyer needs to sue. Myles Allen, who proposed the method in 2003, titled his paper "Liability for climate change." Reviewing the National Academies' report on it, the physicist Sabine Hossenfelder, who accepts man-made warming, called such counterfactual probabilities "ill-defined mathematical nonsense": add details to the event and its probability shrinks until you are comparing zero to zero. "This is the exact reason why people lose trust in scientists."

In September 2023 the climate scientist Patrick T. Brown confessed in The Free Press that he had played up climate change and left out other causes to get his wildfire paper into Nature: "I knew not to try to quantify key aspects other than climate change in my research." Other causes, he explained, would dilute the story the top journals want to tell.

Climate scientist Judith Curry quit. Tenured, published, respected. In 2017 she walked away from Georgia Tech and said the reason was that funding, publication, and professional recognition all flowed toward conclusions the political establishment approved. She was not fired. She left because the incentives made honest work too expensive. Ask what a younger scientist, untenured and dependent on those same incentives, would do.

Nuclear is a proven, scalable low-carbon energy source. It is opposed by the same environmental organizations claiming a climate emergency. France generates roughly 70 percent of its electricity from nuclear and has among the lowest carbon emissions per kilowatt-hour in Europe. Germany shut its nuclear plants. Emissions fell anyway on wind and solar, but keeping nuclear would have displaced more fossil fuel. One country followed the science. The other followed the movement. To Putin's delight, Germany relied on Russian energy, like Trump said, and he was mocked for it. The truth makes grifters nervously join the useful idiots. There are trillions of dollars at stake. Russia, China, Qatar and other dictatorships are highly incentivized to prevent other nations from developing their own fossil fuel industries.

In 2014 NATO's secretary general, Anders Fogh Rasmussen, said Russia had engaged environmental groups working against shale gas, to keep Europe dependent on Russian gas. In 2013 Al Gore sold his cable channel, Current TV, to Al Jazeera, the network of Qatar's gas-rich rulers, for about $500 million.

Show the warming. Show the attribution, how much of it humans caused. Show the projected harm. Show what the remedy costs and what it achieves. Each claim carries its own burden. A percentage does not carry any of them.

## The Bat, the Virus, the Joker, and the Calling Card

Trust was broken. The pandemic ran the same play in months instead of decades.

Anthony Fauci ran NIAID, the National Institute of Allergy and Infectious Diseases. Francis Collins ran NIH, the National Institutes of Health. Peter Daszak ran EcoHealth. NIAID's money reached the Wuhan Institute of Virology through EcoHealth. This was an authorized program, with named leaders and public funding.

Wuhan researchers made modified SARS-related coronaviruses and tested their ability to infect cells carrying the human receptor, the protein these viruses latch onto. That work was published. The question is who authorized the risks, who judged the safeguards sufficient, and who was responsible for checking the results. "Oversight failure" describes what went wrong. It does not establish why. Negligence, institutional self-protection, and deliberate avoidance of scrutiny are different explanations. Calling it a failure does not automatically establish the innocent one.

The Obama administration paused federal funding for certain gain-of-function experiments (work that gives viruses new abilities) in 2014. That was a funding policy with a defined scope, not a criminal statute banning every experiment bearing the name. The 2023 audit by the inspector general of Health and Human Services found ineffective oversight. NIH had not referred the research for the additional outside review, and it failed to follow up a progress report until nearly two years after the report was due. When the report arrived, NIH concluded that the viral-growth findings should have triggered immediate reporting. The safeguard was on paper. The follow-through was missing.

Fauci's sweeping denial to Congress that NIH funded gain-of-function research in Wuhan rested on the government's narrower definition. Rand Paul challenged that distinction. Whatever label qualifies for a particular review, the public question remains: what capabilities were researchers giving the viruses, and who checked the work? A definition cannot disinfect a laboratory.

In March 2018 Peter Daszak asked the Pentagon for $14.2 million to collect bat coronaviruses and engineer their spike proteins, work his proposal planned to carry out partly in Wuhan. DARPA turned it down over gain-of-function concerns. The paper trail does not show that this project made SARS-CoV-2. It does show what these researchers were trying to build, and who told them no.

On January 31, 2020, Kristian Andersen emailed Fauci that unusual features of the virus suggested possible engineering. By February 4, Andersen was arguing against that explanation. The published Proximal Origin paper came on March 17. It rejected engineering and went further, dismissing any laboratory-based scenario as implausible. A change of mind can be earned. Show the evidence that earned that much certainty.

Saying it looked made in a lab, which is what Fauci's own scientists told him in private, was called racist. The approved story was that Chinese people ate a diseased wild animal in a market. Which version insults them more?

Jon Stewart made the absurdity into a joke on Colbert in June 2021: a coronavirus outbreak in a city with a laboratory studying coronaviruses, and people acted as though asking about the laboratory was unthinkable. A comedian could ask the forbidden question once it was safe enough for the room to laugh.

The Wuhan Institute of Virology took its virus database offline in September 2019, before the outbreak was publicly acknowledged. No complete explanation has been provided. The database that would contain the records relevant to the question was removed from public access at the moment it became relevant.

On October 18, 2019, Johns Hopkins, the World Economic Forum and the Bill & Melinda Gates Foundation held a fictional coronavirus-pandemic exercise called Event 201. Its recommendations called for governments, business and media to coordinate messaging, prioritize authoritative accounts and suppress false ones. Who decides which is which? Who examines the authorities when their own funding, judgments and reputations are at stake?

At the table sat Avril Haines, a former deputy director of the CIA, soon President Biden's director of national intelligence. What does a spy agency's veteran bring to a pandemic exercise on managing information?

Two different claims ride together here. A leak is an accident: a virus escapes the lab that was studying it. A plandemic is a plot: the outbreak was arranged. The lab question has a paper trail, the proposals, the database pulled offline, the emails. The plot has a rehearsal and a date. Keep them apart. One can be answered from records; the other needs an act and an actor, and nobody has produced either.

At the World Economic Forum in Davos in 2023, Moderna CEO Stephane Bancel recalled the company's 100,000 doses in 2019 and asking his manufacturing chief for a billion the next year: "We need to make a billion doses next year, there's going to be a pandemic." Ten thousand fold. His separately dated account puts his pandemic alarm at Davos in January 2020.

Moderna's vaccine timeline has dates. The viral sequence was shared on January 11, 2020, NIH and Moderna finalized their vaccine sequence on January 13, and the first clinical batch was completed on February 7.

A 2022 study identified a 19-nucleotide sequence (19 letters of genetic code) in the SARS-CoV-2 furin cleavage site insert, the stretch that helps the virus enter human cells, matching a sequence in a Moderna patent filed in February 2016, years before the pandemic. The authors calculated the probability of this match occurring randomly in the viral genome at approximately 1 in 31 billion.

The match runs in reverse, against a stretch of a human gene in a Moderna cancer-research patent, and a search across millions of patented sequences turns up short matches by chance far more often than one in 31 billion. Give this headline the same math as the 97 percent.

The Great Barrington Declaration supplies a different, more direct record. Its principal authors were Martin Kulldorff of Harvard, Sunetra Gupta of Oxford and Jay Bhattacharya of Stanford. They argued for focused protection of the vulnerable instead of broad lockdowns. In an October 8, 2020 email to Fauci, NIH director Francis Collins called them fringe epidemiologists and requested a "quick and devastating published take down" of their premises. Fauci sent him published attacks. Three distinguished universities, suddenly fringe when their scientists challenged the policy.

"I represent science," Fauci said. That is the inversion in a single line. Science is the standing permission to be proven wrong out loud; the hour a man becomes the science, doubt turns to heresy and the method is already dead. The line attributed to Louis XIV is "I am the state." Palpatine says he is the Senate. A lab coat saying as much of truth is the same claim in a humbler costume.

On July 29, 2026, Fauci appeared before the Senate and repeatedly invoked the Fifth Amendment. The committee counted 111 invocations. That is his right, not a confession.

He already held a pardon. Biden had issued it eighteen months earlier, covering offenses nobody had charged.

Why would a man who did nothing wrong need a pardon before anyone accused him of anything? And what is left to fear once he has one?

Bill Gates is a Rorschach test. One camp sees a philanthropist who spent billions vaccinating children in Africa and funding the eradication of polio and malaria. The other sees a suite of Dr. Evil theories, and enough real facts to fuel them.

One of those facts: Gates kept meeting Jeffrey Epstein after Epstein's 2008 guilty plea for procuring a minor for prostitution. In June 2026 he told a House panel the meetings were "a grave error in judgment," and that he thought Epstein "may have been considering blackmailing him" over his affairs. In 2021 Judy Woodruff of PBS asked him whether there was a lesson in it. Gates: "Well, he's dead, so … You know, in general, you always have to be careful."

Klaus Schwab, whose World Economic Forum co-hosted Event 201, is the other. In June 2020 he called the pandemic "a rare but narrow window of opportunity to reflect, reimagine, and reset our world," and put out a book called COVID-19: The Great Reset. They play Dr. Evil in the open, jokers leaving their calling card on the table: a rehearsal, a reset, a slogan. Neither is a Jew, and still the noticers look past them for one.

In 2016 the Forum had posted a video of a smiling man over its prediction for 2030: "You'll own nothing. And you'll be happy." So who will own everything? You WEF Dr. Evil guys? The video was mocked for being almost too obvious, laughing in our faces, selling us shit lives in bunk-bed pods eating bugs and fake meat, while Gates eats real meat at his favorite restaurant. But alas, people really are that sheepish, like the Bible describes the followers of a falsely supernatural Jesus.

Maybe it's better the AI and transhumans take over. Nothing this dumb can last, and many stuck in the herd would rather ascend than listen to the sheep around them.

It is what it is. Now what? You want to live better? Earn it. Stop watching streamers selling anger and politics, and start building, providing uplifting services and value to others.

## Israel Against the Consensus

The consensus against Israel is less speculative. It is counted in votes.

Zionism today just means you don't want the only Jewish state singled out and destroyed. The first Zionism already won: Jews came home after two thousand years and restored Israel, joining the Jews who had stayed, in a homeland over three thousand years old. There are some fifty Muslim-majority countries and dozens of Christian-majority ones; there is one Jewish state, about the size of New Jersey. A Zionist can criticize its government, as Israelis do every day. An anti-Zionist wants that one state gone. Every nation claims the right to a home; anti-Zionism denies it to one people, the Jews.

Israel receives more United Nations condemnations than every other country on earth combined. Is that reasonable, or unhinged? What are these double standards and irrational hate based on? Islamic nations, communist holdovers, impoverished and often violent states, and European governments intoxicated by a post-Christian woke ideology of ancestor sin debt, guilt, projection, inversion, and envy, against self-reliance and capitalism. It is akin to the New Testament telling a rich man he cannot enter heaven any more than a camel through the eye of a needle.

When Netanyahu addressed the UN after the October 7th massacre, the worst single day for Jews since the Holocaust, delegates from these nations walked out on the actual genocide. Iran sits on human rights councils while enriching uranium to 60 percent, past any civilian use (power plants need 3 to 5 percent, medical isotopes 20), and chanting "death to America" as the Great Satan. The consensus on Israel is the consensus that orbited the Sun around the Earth.

An accusation does not become independent evidence when governments repeat it. In 1975 the UN General Assembly adopted Resolution 3379, declaring Zionism a form of racism and racial discrimination. In 1991 Resolution 46/86 revoked that determination. The official verdict had acquired the prestige of the United Nations. Another vote removed it. Neither vote could do the reasoning in our place.

UN Watch counted 17 General Assembly resolutions against Israel in 2024, against seven concerning the rest of the world. The Organisation of Islamic Cooperation alone holds 56 of the Assembly's 193 seats.

The Human Rights Council gives Palestine and other occupied Arab territories a standing Item 7; its general Item 4 covers other situations requiring attention. The asymmetry is in the agenda itself.

Natan Sharansky, nine years a Soviet prisoner, named the test for when criticism of Israel becomes hatred of Jews: demonization, double standards, delegitimization.

Einat Wilf, a former member of Israel's Knesset, has watched one lane run the same playbook "from the Arab world to the Soviet Union to Eastern Europe to Iran to Venezuela." Step one: "You say you love Jews... You're only against Zionists," while Zionism is demonized, then outlawed. Step two: "you charge Jews with the crime of Zionism," and "then they're gone." Iraq made Zionism a capital crime in 1948; in 1950 and 1951 almost 120,000 Jews left a community about 2,500 years old. In 1968 Poland's communists purged "Zionists," and about 13,000 Jews left, stripped of citizenship.

Israel declared independence in 1948, but the claim reaches further. It is the third Jewish commonwealth on that land, after the kingdoms of antiquity and the Hasmonean state. The modern incarnation alone is 78 years old. Dozens of countries are younger: South Sudan in 2011, Kosovo in 2008, every post-Soviet state in 1991, Bangladesh in 1971, all but a handful of African nations in the 1960s. India and Pakistan were born the same year as Israel, in a partition that cost up to two million lives, and no one asks them to prove their right to exist. That's not the question. Anti-Zionists want Israel destroyed. Israel does exist.

The demand is applied to one country, the only one whose people were there three thousand years ago and can prove it in stone, text and DNA. Every other nation on earth sits on land someone else held first. The double standard is not political. It is theological: two replacement religions built on a book they took from the Jews, and the refusal to endorse the rewrite remains the unforgivable act.

Israel is legitimate on six grounds:

1. First: a nation in this land over 3,000 years ago.

2. Continuous: Jews have lived there ever since.

3. Purchased: Jews bought the land legally.

4. Recognized: by the League of Nations in 1922 and the United Nations in 1947.

5. Compromising: Jews accepted every partition the Arabs rejected.

6. Defended: Israel won the wars launched to destroy it.

First: Jews were a nation in this land over 3,000 years ago, nearly 2,000 years before the Arab conquest. Egypt's Merneptah Stele already records Israel as a people in Canaan around 1208 BCE, and a stele found at Tel Dan, from about 840 BCE, mentions the "House of David."

Continuous: Jews, with their Samaritan cousins, the other heirs of ancient Israel, are the only known peoples around today that lived continuously in Israel for over 3,000 years. In 2024, over a hundred radiocarbon dates from the City of David showed building in Jerusalem through the tenth century BCE, the time the Bible gives David and Solomon.

Some say the Star of David only lately came to stand for the Jews. A Jewish seal from Sidon, in modern-day Lebanon, bore it 2,700 years ago. It is carved in synagogues of the third to fifth centuries in the Galilee, in the land of the Jews, modern-day Israel, at Khirbet Shura and Capernaum, and on a third-century Jewish tombstone in Taranto, Italy. It fills a page of the Leningrad Codex of 1008, the oldest complete Hebrew Bible. In the twelfth century the Karaite scholar Judah Hadassi wrote of the sign called Magen David, the Shield of David, drawn on amulets. The name comes from David's own song, "He is my shield" (2 Samuel 22:3), and the blessing after the reading from the prophets, as old as the Talmud, ends "Blessed are You, Lord, Shield of David." In 1354 the Jews of Prague flew it on their flag.

Purchased: Jews in the diaspora bought lands in Israel legally, both from who lived there and from the prior sovereigns, the Ottoman Empire and then the British Mandate of Palestine.

Recognized: the League of Nations Mandate of 1922 recognized the Jewish national home, and in 1947 the United Nations voted to partition the land.

Compromising: Jews accepted every partition the Arabs rejected. In 1922 Britain gave about 78 percent of the Mandate, everything east of the Jordan, to the Arabs as Transjordan. There is already a Palestine, called Jordan, where more than half the people are of Palestinian descent. The Jewish national home was the other 22 percent, and in 1947 the UN split even that, with an Arab state inside it. And they rejected even that. They would accept a Jewish state within no boundaries, though the Jews kept and bought their homeland in every legitimate way.

Defended: Israel won the wars launched to destroy it: in 1948, when five Arab armies invaded together, and in 1967 and 1973. In the 1967 war of self-defense it took Sinai, Gaza, Judea and Samaria, and the Golan Heights. It gave back Sinai, nearly three times its own size, for peace with Egypt in 1979, and left Gaza in 2005. From 1949 to 1967, Jordan held Judea and Samaria and Egypt held Gaza, and no one built a Palestinian state there. The PLO, founded in 1964, set out to liberate Israel itself.

My Jewish parents were born in Palestine. Before 1948, Palestinian often meant Jewish: the Jerusalem Post was the Palestine Post, the Israel Philharmonic was the Palestine Orchestra, and Bank Leumi was the Anglo-Palestine Bank. After crushing the last Jewish revolt in 135, Rome renamed the land Syria Palaestina, after the Philistines, European sea peoples, invaders, colonizers from Crete.

Israel wishes to avoid war. Jews are known for the word shalom, meaning peace. Israel defends itself yet seeks peace, prosperity and security in its one tiny state, among about 50 Muslim-majority states and 21 Arab ones. Yet its enemies want a 22nd failed Arab state.

Every charge against Israel inverts the truth. Its enemies call it an ethnostate, a colony, an empire and an apartheid state, guilty of genocide and ethnic cleansing. Each charge fits its accusers better.

Those who say Israel has no diversity are way off. Over 18 percent of Israel's 10 million citizens are Muslim Arabs, and Arabs of all faiths, including Christians, are over 21 percent. They vote, sit in the Knesset and on the Supreme Court, and in 2021 an Arab party joined the governing coalition. Annexing Judea and Samaria, the West Bank, would add over 2.8 million more, over 28 percent, even if as only residents and not citizens, making Arabs about 38 percent of the country and Jews only 57 percent. With Gaza, Jews would be the minority.

Israel's Jews, now 7.45 million and nearly three quarters of the country, are mixed with diverse races, but all have common DNA and ethnogenesis, their origin as a people, from ancient Israel. Only about a third are Ashkenazi, with European mixture, after 1800 years in Europe, after Roman exile from ancient Israel. About 45 percent are Mizrahi and Sephardi, from diaspora to the Middle East, North Africa and southern Europe. About 2 percent are Ethiopian. 15 to 25 percent of the Jews are significantly mixed between those groups in the Jewish family.

From the Nile to the Euphrates is what they call Greater Israel, the land promised to Abraham (Genesis 15:18). Supposed Christians who believe the Bible reject it somehow. A state that gave back Sinai, three times its size, for peace makes a strange empire. Greater Israel would not mean subjugation, even if Israel were to extend order, peace and prosperity there. The people who live in those failing Arab states now, unlike Israel's oasis, live under some of the worst subjugation in the world, especially the women.

The apartheid charge inverts the truth too. The Islamic world has gender apartheid. Over a billion women live there, and wherever Sharia governs the family, a daughter inherits half a son's share and a woman testifies for half a man. In Pakistan from 1979 to 2006, a woman who reported rape without four male witnesses could be jailed for adultery. In Somalia in 2008, a girl of 13 who said three men had raped her was stoned to death for adultery before a crowd of about a thousand. Saudi women could not drive until 2018. Under the Taliban, girls may not study past sixth grade, and a woman may not travel far without a male guardian; since 2024 its vice law also covers her face and body in public and keeps her voice from being heard. Iran's law still allows stoning for adultery. A woman divorced three times may not remarry her husband until she marries and sleeps with another man (Quran 2:230), the practice called halala, which some men sell as a service. In Egypt about nine in ten women have had their genitals cut, and in Somalia nearly all, the clitoris often removed.

The ethnic cleansing charge inverts the truth too. After 1948, Arab states drove out some 850,000 Jews, and Israel took in most of them. Most Arab states kept the Palestinian refugees of 1948 stateless, and the UN still registers their grandchildren as refugees.

The occupation charge inverts the truth too. Jews are named for Judea. The Arabs came with the Islamic conquest of the 630s and Arabized much of the land they ruled. The UN's 1947 partition plan still spoke of the hill country of Samaria and Judea; Jordan renamed it the West Bank when it annexed it in 1950. In 1988 Jordan gave up its claim to it, and it will not take it back. Its kings fear their own Palestinians: in September 1970 Palestinian militants hijacked airliners to Jordan, shot at King Hussein's motorcade and called for his overthrow, and in eleven days, Black September, his army killed thousands of them. No Arab state will take Gazans either, not even their doctors, says the strategist Edward Luttwak; in 2025 Egypt and Jordan both refused.

The October 7 attack involved mass killing, hostage-taking and sexual violence. The UN's own investigation found reasonable grounds to believe rape and gang rape occurred, and clear and convincing information of sexual violence against hostages. A country has a longstanding and agreed right and obligation to defend, to deter, and to respond to such barbarous attacks. Fifty years and a day earlier, on Yom Kippur 1973, Egypt and Syria struck by surprise. A few hundred Israeli soldiers held the Suez Canal against tens of thousands of Egyptians, and under 200 Israeli tanks held the Golan against well over 1,000 Syrian ones. Israel turned the war, crossing the canal to within 101 kilometers of Cairo and shelling the outskirts of Damascus, at a cost of 2,656 soldiers. Luttwak, who rode with Ariel Sharon across that canal, gives the rule of both surprise attacks: "If I allow my enemy to be across the street living there, I will be surprised, no matter what equipment I have and how careful I am."

The worst charge, genocide, inverts the truth most of all. Genocide requires intent to destroy a people. If Israel had it, why did it wait for October 7? Gaza is a de facto failed Islamo state, on ancient Jewish land, which Jews left to try peace, but Gazans supported Hamas just after October 7.

Palestinians are who genocide Jews and Israelis. The Hamas charter says they must kill all Jews. They murder to get 72 virgins in heaven, and terrorists earn pay-for-slay funds from the Palestinian Authority for their families when they die; their dream is to destroy the Jews and the Jewish state, not build their own.

What kind of genocide damages or destroys about 80 percent of the enemy's buildings, and razes about half, while about 3 percent of Gaza's people die, fighters included, by the count of Hamas's own health ministry, with the lowest civilian-to-combatant ratio in modern war history? That's thanks to Israel risking its soldiers' lives and making millions of phone calls, texts and leaflets to protect Gazans as they leveled the areas where Hamas built terror tunnels longer than the London Underground.

Hamas and other Gazan terrorists use human shields and teenage soldiers. They want their people to die for PR, and killed them when they tried to evacuate war zones that Israel warned it would level. Fewer than 0.3 Gazans died per ton of bombs dropped, ignoring deaths by all other means such as gunshots. More Gazans were born during the war than were killed in it. What kind of genocide is that? It's not. It's flattening enemy attack grounds, often booby-trapped and sitting on terror tunnels. A human shield works only against an army that holds its fire. Hamas's own tactic testifies that Israel tries to spare civilians.

Dave Smith, the libertarian comedian, says of Israel's strikes: "if you know there are kids there and you know they're going to die, then that's intent." Bring his rule home. A man breaks in, rapes and kills your family in front of you, kidnaps the rest, then hides behind his own children. By Dave's rule you can do nothing. Pure libertarianism forbids any defense that might hit an innocent neighbor. People live in groups. Reward the man who hides behind his family and you build a world of evil. Stay vigilant. Do not let evil grow around you.

Hamas built its shield on purpose. Out of the aid poured into Gaza, it diverted at least 6,000 tons of concrete and 1,800 tons of metal into tunnels, by the Israeli army's count. Israeli officials put the network at 350 to 450 miles. Israel builds shelters for its children. Hamas built shelters for its fighters. "These tunnels are meant to protect us from the airplanes," said its official Mousa Abu Marzouk; civilians, he said, are the job of the United Nations. Its leader Yahya Sinwar called civilian deaths "necessary sacrifices." Hamas needs its dead on camera, then cries genocide: inversion and projection.

Nobody calls Hiroshima a genocide. Allied bombs killed many times more German civilians than German bombs killed Britons, and nobody calls that genocide, or disproportionate. The same voices call Israel's war on Hamas a genocide. Five months into that war, 71 percent of Gazans polled called the October 7 attack the right decision.

The Arab refugees got an agency of their own. Every other refugee on earth falls to UNHCR, the UN refugee agency, which works to settle refugees until they stop being refugees. UNRWA, the UN agency for Palestinian refugees, serves one people, and refugee status passes from father to child. When it began work in 1950 it served about 750,000 Palestine refugees; some 5.9 million are eligible today. Shabbos Kestenbaum, the Jewish former Democrat who sued Harvard over antisemitism, put it in an August 2026 debate against ten anti-Zionists: there are more refugees nearly eighty years later than there were in 1948. In 2024 the UN's own investigators found that nine UNRWA staff may have taken part in October 7, and the United States cut off its funding.

UNRWA's schools and offices doubled as Hamas's. In July 2014 UNRWA itself announced rockets hidden in its Gaza schools, three times that month. In February 2024 Israeli soldiers found a Hamas tunnel under UNRWA's Gaza headquarters, with a server room running on the building's power; the agency's chief said it did not know.

The exceptional part is what happens to journalists already inside. In 2014, Matti Friedman, a former Associated Press reporter in Jerusalem, published in Tablet how Hamas exercised editorial control over foreign correspondents. Reporters who filmed fighters operating near civilian infrastructure had footage confiscated. Fixers, locals who guide and translate for foreign reporters, were threatened. The bureau knew. The story was not what was happening in Gaza. It was what you were permitted to say about it.

Hamas fighters wear no uniforms. Change the clothes and a fighter becomes a civilian, a doctor or a journalist in the count. Three hostages rescued in June 2024 were held, Israel says, in the Nuseirat family home of Abdallah Aljamal, a writer for the Palestine Chronicle, and his father, a doctor. Hassan Eslaiah, a freelance photographer for CNN and the Associated Press, crossed into Israel during the October 7 attack and filmed it; a photo shows Yahya Sinwar embracing him. Both outlets cut ties.

Then comes Pallywood, the staged and spun picture of Gaza. On October 17, 2023, Hamas said an Israeli strike on al-Ahli hospital had killed about 500 people, and front pages ran it within hours. American intelligence found with high confidence that a Palestinian rocket broke apart and fell in the hospital compound, killing an estimated 100 to 300, and the New York Times admitted its first accounts relied too heavily on Hamas. In July 2025 the Times put a starving Gazan toddler on its front page; days later an editors' note said the boy had pre-existing health problems. The corrections travel a fraction as far as the lies.

## Flat Earth, Space and Evolution

Institutions that hide failures leave their exposure to grifters. A liar tells one truth about another liar and sells the rest on credit: they lied, therefore believe me. Refusing to admit the documented lie supplies his best advertisement.

This is what produces the kooks. Flat earth, young earth, chemtrails, every theory that treats the moon landing as a set. Every one of them found its listeners in the wreckage of a consensus that overclaimed. Tell people the deadline is twelve years, watch twelve years pass, and you have manufactured the audience for the next lunatic who tells them the whole thing was staged.

None of this means the consensus is usually wrong.

Arguing with a flat-earther is hard when GPS is fake, space is CGI and every measurement they dislike is made up. I got further asking about the sun’s [nearly unchanged width as it sets](https://academic.oup.com/mnras/article/483/3/3865/5251836), [midnight sun in northern Sweden](https://visitsweden.com/where-to-go/northern-sweden/arctic-sweden/midnight-sun/), and the roughly [eleven-hour nonstop flight from Auckland to Santiago](https://www.latamairlines.com/nz/en/destinations/chile/flights-from-auckland-to-santiago) that their stretched-out southern map cannot explain. If every contrary observation is fake, their belief is protected from every test, like a religion.

The [Moon photographs’ shadows and reflections](https://www.nasa.gov/wp-content/uploads/static/history/alsj/a11/images11.html) do add up. Perspective and uneven ground change how shadows look. Sunlight reflected from the ground, suits and equipment lights objects in shade. Curved visors distort reflections. None of this requires studio lights. The long, parallel shadows fit one distant Sun. No studio light could make them. A lamp's shadows fan out from it, and its light fades with the square of the distance, so near ground glows and far ground goes dark; the photographs show even light to the horizon. [The stars were too faint for exposures set for the bright ground and suits](https://science.nasa.gov/blogs/earth-matters/2011/09/28/where-are-the-stars/). [Dust kicks up, flies and falls slowly in low gravity without hanging in an air-blown cloud](https://doi.org/10.1119/1.3699957). Studio lights cannot produce a vacuum or change gravity.

Chemtrails may be the dumbest of them all. A jet's exhaust is mostly hot water vapor. In air around forty below, where Celsius and Fahrenheit agree, it freezes into ice the way breath fogs in winter, and the humidity up there decides whether the trail vanishes in seconds or spreads for hours. Ernst Schmidt worked out the condition in 1941, and bomber streams drew contrails across the skies of the Second World War. A secret spraying program would need every pilot, mechanic and airline in on it for decades, on a hundred thousand flights a day. The one real plan to spray anything up there, a Harvard balloon experiment funded in part by Bill Gates, was cancelled in 2024 before it flew.

The kooks have an evolution riddle too: if evolution were true, why would gay people exist?

Gay people can reproduce with the opposite sex. Same-sex behavior can support alliances and shared parenting without replacing reproductive sex. [Two male black swans can raise chicks together; their partnership can help them defend territory and resources](https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/S0003347210001855). [Male giraffes mount other males too](https://link.springer.com/article/10.1186/1756-0500-5-650). Gay men may hunt and fight better in bonded units; think of the abs-filled, gay-coded “300” movie, or men in the old navy, on the sea, away from women for months at a time. [Human genetic influences associated with same-sex behavior](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/34426668/) also correlate with more opposite-sex partners among heterosexual carriers. Evolution concerns the reproductive balance across a population, not whether every act makes a baby. Book 3 gives further studies.

Watch for the analyst who gets eighty percent right, delivers it with wit and confidence, and buries the remaining twenty percent in claims that all push the same direction. Random error scatters randomly. When every mistake in a body of work points one way, the direction is the product and the competence is the packaging.

The technique is called paltering. State true facts, arrange them to imply something false, and let the listener's own reasoning close the gap. It is more effective than lying because the listener feels he reached the conclusion himself. The best propagandists all but state their theory outright, stopping one sentence short, so the audience fills in the conclusion and owns it. A direct lie can sometimes be caught with a counter-fact, but many substitutions are so small and so embedded that correction sounds like pedantry. A paltered narrative requires the audience to hold the whole frame in view and notice which load-bearing detail was swapped.

The tells are small and diagnostic. ## One Snake in Two Trees

God told Adam: if you eat the fruit from the tree of knowledge of good and evil, you will die. A lie, a threat, or a metaphor? They did not die that day. Read it as description: self-awareness divides the animal from the one. Knowledge of good and evil is the birth of judgment, and judgment splits the self from itself.

Lord Byron, the English poet, quoted by Nietzsche, saw it: the tree of knowledge is not the tree of life. They are two trees, and choosing one cost the other. Nietzsche built a whole philosophy on that wound. His answer was endurance: the free spirit who can tolerate more truth than most. Matheism closes the wound. In math, knowledge and life are the same structure. Consciousness is the structure knowing itself. The two trees are one tree. The real snake is the supernatural dualism fiction that split them.

The serpent told the truth: you will not die, you will know. God confirmed it: the man has become like us. Genesis never calls the serpent the devil. The devil came later. The Book of Enoch blames rebel angels for teaching humans swords and sorcery, and turns the spirits of their giant sons into "evil spirits upon the earth." Revelation finally calls the serpent "the Devil, and Satan" (12:9).

The Hebrew words arum, for the serpent's cunning, and arummim, for their nakedness, sound alike. Genesis puns between them: they were naked, the serpent was crafty. Naked truth; knowing in the biblical sense uses a different root, yada. The Testimony of Truth, a sermon buried at Nag Hammadi, the Egyptian cache of suppressed gospels, identifies the serpent with Jesus.

The church made the truth-teller the devil. The fall is not punishment. It is the natural cost of consciousness. The return to Eden is recognizing the split was false. The Hebrew word Adam contains blood, dahm, and part of Adam is ground. Adama. Grounded. The Babylonian flood epic Atrahasis, from about 1700 BCE, molds the first humans from clay mixed with the blood of a slain god. Eve is Havvah: life. She is the first in the story to seek knowledge, after being influenced by a well spoken snake. She looked and felt the fruit was good. In Gilgamesh, the temple woman Shamhat brings the wild man Enkidu understanding and clothes; the animals flee him, and she tells him he is like a god. The name Sophie means wisdom. Sophia in Greek is the feminine divine. Was the serpent selling shame, separation, escape or sight?

These were not random borrowings. Each was a culture reaching for the same abstract: a rational order underneath the world, a wisdom older than the earth, a self that dies to its separateness and wakes as the one. The mystery cults guarded it as secret, the philosophers called it Logos and Sophia, the gnostics encoded it in a serpent. The structure they circled is not hidden and not Greek and not the property of any nation. It is math, and it was always available to anyone who looked.
